Crate
nl_

Source
Expand description
nl-compiler
An experimental library for HDL frontends that can compile to
safety-net
netlists.
Structs
§
Verilog
Error
Errors for Verilog Compilation.
Enums
§
AigError
Errors for AIG Compilation.
Traits
§
FromId
A trait to specify how to map primitive instantiation names (
Identifier
s) to the instance
safety_net::Instantiable
type.
Functions
§

aig

Netlist
from a AIG

pub enum DataType {
TwoState,
ThreeState,
FourState,
}
Expand description
Signals in a circuit can be binary, tri-state, or four-state.
Variants
§
§
TwoState
A logical 0 or 1
§
ThreeState
A logical 0, 1, or high-Z
§
FourState
A logical 0, 1, high-Z, or unknown (X)
Implementations

pub enum Error {

Expand description
Errors for the
safety-net
library.
Variants
§
§
CycleDetected(
Vec
<
Net
>)
Error for an analysis cannot run due to cycles.
§
ParseError(
String
)
Errors in parsing literals/identifiers.
§
NonuniqueNets(
Vec
<
Net
>)
The labeled nets in the netlist are not unique.
§
NonuniqueInsts(
Vec
<
Identifier
>)
The labeled instances in the netlist are not unique.
§
NoOutputs
The netlist has no outputs.
§
InstantiableError(
String
)
An error in the instantiable interface
§
DanglingReference(
Vec
<
Net
>)
A deletion would cause a dangling reference.
§

usize
,
usize
)
Mismatch in number of arguments
§
InputNeedsAlias(
Net
)
An input needs an alias to be an output
§
NetNotFound(
Net
)
A net that was expected but not found
§
TypeError(
Net
)
A connection does not type check

pub enum Logic {
False,
True,
X,
Z,
}
Expand description
An enum to represent four-state logic
Variants
§
§
False
Logical zero
§
True
Logical one
§
X
Don’t care
§
Z
High-impedance state

pub enum Object<I>
where
I:
Instantiable
,
{
Input(
Net
),
Instance(
Vec
<
Net
>,
Identifier
, I),
}
Expand description
A tagged union for objects in a digital circuit, which can be either an input net or an instance of a module or primitive.
Variants
§
§
Input(
Net
)
A principal input to the circuit
§
Instance(
Vec
<
Net
>,
Identifier
, I)
An instance of a module or primitive
Implementations

pub enum CombDepthResult {
Undefined,
CombCycle,
Depth(
usize
),
}
Expand description
A simple example to analyze the logic levels of a netlist.
This analysis checks for cycles, but it doesn’t check for registers.
Result of combinational depth analysis for a single net.
Variants
§
§
Undefined
Signal has no driver
§
CombCycle
Signal is along a cycle
§
Depth(
usize
)
Integer logic level

Enums
§
Comb
Depth
Result
A simple example to analyze the logic levels of a netlist.
This analysis checks for cycles, but it doesn’t check for registers.
Result of combinational depth analysis for a single net.
Traits
§
Analysis
A common trait of analyses than can be performed on a netlist.
An analysis becomes stale when the netlist is modified.

pub struct CombDepthInfo<'a, I:
Instantiable
> {
/* private fields */
}
Expand description
Computes the combinational depth of each net in a netlist.
Each net is classified as having a defined depth, being undefined,
or participating in a combinational cycle.

pub trait Analysis<'a, I:

>

Netlist

Expand description
A common trait of analyses than can be performed on a netlist.
An analysis becomes stale when the netlist is modified.
Required Methods
§
Source

build
(netlist: &'a
Netlist
<I>) ->
Result
<Self,
Error
>

Dyn Compatibility
§
This trait is
not
dyn compatible
.
In older versions of Rust, dyn compatibility was called "object safety".

safety_net - Rust

Crate safety_net

net

Modules

Functions
Type Aliases
Crate
safety_
net
Copy item path
Source
Expand description
safety-net
An experimental library for representing circuit netlists for EDA tool development.
Take a look at some
examples
and the
documentation
.
The most important API is the
Netlist
struct.
§
Simple Example
use
safety_net::{Gate, Netlist};
fn
and_gate() -> Gate {
Gate::new_logical(
"AND"
.into(),
vec!
[
"A"
.into(),
"B"
.into()],
"Y"
.into())
}
fn
main() {
let
netlist = Netlist::new(
"example"
.into());
// Add the the two inputs
let
a = netlist.insert_input(
"a"
.into());
let
b = netlist.insert_input(
"b"
.into());
// Instantiate an AND gate
let
instance = netlist
.insert_gate(and_gate(),
"inst_0"
.into(),
&
[a, b])
.unwrap();
// Make this AND gate an output
instance.expose_with_name(
"y"
.into());
// Print the netlist
println!
(
"{netlist}"
);
}
Re-exports
§
pub use graph::
Analysis
;
pub use graph::
FanOutTable
;

module.

§
assert_
verilog_
eq

filter_
nodes
Filter invariants of
Instantiable
in a netlist. Use it like you would
matches!
.
Example:
filter_nodes!(netlist, Gate::AND(_));
format_
id
Functions like the
format!
macro, but returns an
Identifier
Structs
§
Attribute
An attribute can add information to instances and wires in string form, like ‘dont_touch’
Attribute
Filter
Filter nodes/nets in the netlist by some attribute, like “dont_touch”
Connection
Represent a driven net alongside its connection to an input port
Driven
Net
Represent a net that is being driven by a
Instantiable
Gate
A primitive gate in a digital circuit, such as AND, OR, NOT, etc.
VDD and GND are reserved to represent logic one and zero, respectively.
Identifier
An identifier of a node in a circuit
Input
Port
Represent the input port of a primitive
Net
A net in a circuit, which is identified with a name and data type.
NetRef
Provides an idiomatic interface
to the interior mutability of the netlist
Netlist
A netlist data structure
Enums

Data
Type
Signals in a circuit can be binary, tri-state, or four-state.
Error
Errors for the
safety-net
library.
Logic
An enum to represent four-state logic
Object
A tagged union for objects in a digital circuit, which can be either an input net or an instance of a module or primitive.
Parameter
A dedicated type to parameters for instantiables
Traits
§
Instantiable
A trait for primitives in a digital circuit, such as gates or other components.

DFSIterator
Copy item path
Source
pub struct DFSIterator<'a, I:
Instantiable
> {
/* private fields */
}
Expand description
A depth-first iterator over the circuit nodes in a netlist
§
Examples
use
safety_net::iter::DFSIterator;
use
safety_net::GateNetlist;
let
netlist = GateNetlist::new(
"example"
.into());
netlist.insert_input(
"input1"
.into());
let
mut
nodes = Vec::new();
let
mut
dfs = DFSIterator::new(
&
netlist, netlist.last().unwrap());
while let
Some
(n) = dfs.next() {
if
dfs.check_cycles() {
panic!
(
"Cycle detected in the netlist"
);
}
nodes.push(n);
}

pub struct NetIterator<'a, I:

safety_net
Module
rewriter
Copy item path
Source
Expand description
Strategies for fast batching netlist rewrites
Structs
§
NetMapper
Uses a union-find to batch replacements in a netlist closer to O(n) time.
The replacement only considers the uses of a net that existed at the time of creation of
NetMapper
.
Connections created after the creation of the
NetMapper
will not be replaced.

Source
pub struct NetMapper<'a, I:
Instantiable
> {
/* private fields */
}
Expand description
Uses a union-find to batch replacements in a netlist closer to O(n) time.
The replacement only considers the uses of a net that existed at the time of creation of
NetMapper
.
Connections created after the creation of the
NetMapper
will not be replaced.
Implementations

pub fn
expose_with_name

Exposes this driven net as a top-level output with a specific port name.
Multiple calls to this method can be used to create multiple output aliases for the same net.

Gate

Source
pub struct Gate {
/* private fields */
}
Expand description
A primitive gate in a digital circuit, such as AND, OR, NOT, etc.
VDD and GND are reserved to represent logic one and zero, respectively.

if the primitive has no input ports. In most cases, this means the cell represents a constant.
This method should be overriden if the implemenation of
get_input_ports()
is expensive.

pub fn
rename_nets
<F:
Fn
(&
Identifier
,
usize
) ->
Identifier
>(
&self,
f: F,
) ->
Result
<
()
,
Error
>
Rename nets and instances in the netlist using the provided
injective
function.
Returns an error if the function is not injective.
§
Examples
use
safety_net::format_id;
use
safety_net::{Gate, GateNetlist};
let
netlist = GateNetlist::new(
"example"
.into());
let
inv = Gate::new_logical(
"INV"
.into(),
vec!
[
"A"
.into()],
"Y"
.into());
let
foo = netlist.insert_input(
"foo"
.into());
let
nr = netlist.insert_gate(inv,
"bar"
.into(),
&
[foo]).unwrap();
nr.expose_with_name(
"baz"
.into());
netlist.rename_nets(|id, i|
format_id!
(
"{}_{}"
, id, i) ).unwrap();
// "bar_Y" -> "bar_Y_0"
// "bar" -> "bar_1"

pub enum Error<'a, I:
Instantiable
> {
PassError(&'a dyn
Pass
<I = I>,

),

Pattern
<I = I>,

),

),

Pass
<I = I>,
Error
)
A netlist error in running a pass.

Pattern
<I = I>,
Error
)

§

Error
)
Any other netlist error.

Register passes in a wrapper enum for CLI arg parsing.
Passes which would like to be parameterized should define a tuple struct with a public PhantomData field.

register_passes in safety_pass - Rust
Skip to main content
register_passes
safety_
pass
0.4.17
register_
passes
Sections
See example:
Example
In crate safety_
pass
safety_pass
Macro
register_
passes
Copy item path
Source
macro_rules! register_passes {
($e:ident < $i:ty > ; $($(#[$meta:meta])* $pass:ident $(<$pass_ty:ty>)?),+ $(,)?) => { ... };
}
Expand description
Register passes in a wrapper enum for CLI arg parsing.
Passes which would like to be parameterized should define a tuple struct with a public PhantomData field.
§
See example:
PrintVerilog
§
Example
use
safety_pass::register_passes;
use
safety_pass::{Pass, Cell};
use
safety_pass::passes::PrintVerilog;
// This defines a enum called `BasicPasses` with unit variants.
// They operate on netlists containing `Cell` cells.
register_passes!
(BasicPasses<Cell>;
/// A dummy pass that emits the Verilog of the netlist.
PrintVerilog<Cell>);

Source
pub enum BasicPasses {
CellStats,
Clean,
DedupConsts,
DotGraph,
ExtractInvClock,
FoldAllPatterns,
InsertInv,
InsertScanChain,
ListNets,
PrintVerilog,
RenameNets,
StripAttributes,
}
Expand description
Enum containing all registered passes for argument parsing.
Variants
§
§
CellStats
Prints stats on all the cell types in the netlist.
§
Clean
A pass that cleans the netlist.
§
DedupConsts
Deduplicate constant nets in the netlist.
§
DotGraph
A pass that prints the dot graph of the netlist.
§
ExtractInvClock
Inverts the clock of a cell that has a
IS_CLK_INVERTED
param
§
FoldAllPatterns
A pass that runs all built-in patterns to a fixed point.
§
InsertInv
Insert a pair of inverters at every point in the netlist.
§
InsertScanChain
Insert a scan chain along the FDREs
§
ListNets
List all nets in the netlist
§
PrintVerilog
A dummy pass that emits the Verilog of the netlist.
§
RenameNets
Renames nets/instances sequentially
0
,
1
, …
§
StripAttributes
Strip all cell attributes except ’dont_touch
and
keep`
Implementations
§
Source
§

BasicPasses

A pass that runs all patterns to a covergence.

Fold

Source
pub struct FoldAllPatterns;
Expand description
A pass that runs all patterns to a covergence.
Checks patterns in insertion order
AndIdentity, OrIdentity, AndAbsorb, OrAbsorb, NandIdentity, NorIdentity, NandAbsorb, NorAbsorb,
DoubleNegation, Idempotent, MonotoneFold

safety_pass::patterns - Rust

Module patterns
safety_
pass
0.4.17
Module patterns
Module Items
Structs
In crate safety_
pass
safety_pass
Module
patterns
Copy item path
Source
Expand description
Simple cell patterns.
Structs
§
AndAbsorb
A AND 0 = 0 (absorbing element)
AndIdentity
A AND 1 = A (identity element)
Double
Negation
NOT(NOT(A)) = A, INV(INV(A)) = A
Idempotent
A * A = A
Monotone
Fold
Folds monotone AND/OR gate trees (nested homogeneous gates) into a single equivalent (wider) gate.
The fold applies to all AND or all OR gates until final total fan-in = 4 (max-sized gate, i.e. AND4)
Nand
Absorb
NAND(A, 0) = 1 (absorbing element)
Nand
Identity
NAND(A, 1) = NOT(A) (identity element)
NorAbsorb
NOR(A, 1) = 0 (absorbing element)
NorIdentity
NOR(A, 0) = NOT(A) (identity element)
OrAbsorb
A OR 1 = 1 (absorbing element)
OrIdentity
A OR 0 = A (identity element)

Source
pub struct MonotoneFold;
Expand description
Folds monotone AND/OR gate trees (nested homogeneous gates) into a single equivalent (wider) gate.
The fold applies to all AND or all OR gates until final total fan-in = 4 (max-sized gate, i.e. AND4)
